# RELATIONS.md
Every spec↔code relation in the log, and whether it still holds.
Generated by `mix surfex.goldens --write` · gate `relation-status-drift` (HARD) — do not edit; a drift FAILS the gate.

A row per relation. **dangling**: an end changed since it was confirmed (`mix surfex.confirm`). **orphaned**: an end is gone. **planned**: an end doesn't exist yet. **conflicted**: recorded on two branches (`mix surfex.resolve`). For `depends_on`, `refines` and `tests` the first end depends on, refines or tests the other; the other types' ends are in sorted order. `mix surfex.status` is the check; this is the record.

**2955 relations** · current 2546 · dangling 0 · orphaned 0 · conflicted 0 · retired 409 · planned 0 · unimplemented 0 · new 3 · unmet 0 · broken 0 · stale 0 · undeclared 0 · marks 0 · baseline 0 · broken citations 0 · triangle gaps 0

**124 spec units** · sections 48 · blocks 0 · test hints 76
